### [1] [CrossQ: Task-Aligned Cross-Token Conditional Quantization for Late Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19204)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Ramya Manasa Amancherla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CrossQ 是一种针对晚期交互检索器（如 ColBERT）的新型压缩方法，通过在索引时利用轻量级文档上下文来条件化 token 编码，并结合排序对齐的损失函数，在极低的每 token 比特数下显著提升了检索质量，同时大幅减少索引存储足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互检索器（如 ColBERT）检索质量高，但其多向量索引存储开销巨大。标准压缩方法只优化 token 重建误差，而排序效果关键依赖于少量"胜出"token 的分数保持。现有方法未能对齐压缩目标与排序目标。

Method: CrossQ 在索引阶段利用轻量级文档上下文（但不存储）对 token 编码进行条件化，自适应提升文档内有效 token 的保真度。训练采用排序对齐的目标函数，保持候选分数分布并保护难负样本间隔。同时支持轻量微调以进一步提升压缩质量。

Result: 在 2 B/token 下，CrossQ 相比最强的严格足迹匹配量化基线 MRR@10 提升 +0.010，相比最强候选匹配系统参考提升 +0.012。在九个数据集 BEIR 子集上，4 B/token 下的平均 nDCG@10 相比最强候选匹配系统参考提升 +0.009。4 B/token 时实现约 64x 原始 token 存储压缩（含元数据约 61x，保守对齐约 58x）。8 B/token 下轻量微调可保留全精度 ColBERT 约 98% 的 MRR@10。

Conclusion: CrossQ 有效改善了内存受限场景下晚期交互检索的足迹-质量权衡，通过文档上下文条件化编码和排序对齐训练，在极低比特率下保持了接近全精度模型的检索质量，为大规模多向量索引压缩提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrievers like ColBERT achieve high quality but suffer from large multi-vector indices. Standard compression minimizes token reconstruction error, while ranking depends critically on preserving scores of sparse "winner" tokens. We introduce CrossQ, which adaptively improves effective token fidelity within documents by conditioning token codes on lightweight document context computed at indexing time (but not stored). CrossQ is trained with ranking-aligned objectives that preserve candidate score distributions and protect hard-negative margins. At 2 B/token, CrossQ improves MRR@10 by +0.010 over the strongest strictly footprint-matched quantization baseline and by +0.012 over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. On a nine-dataset BEIR subset, CrossQ improves average nDCG@10 by +0.009 at 4 B/token over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. At 4 B/token, CrossQ achieves 64x raw token-storage reduction, approximately 61x including metadata and approximately 58x under conservative padding/alignment accounting. At 8 B/token, CrossQ with light fine-tuning retains approximately 98% of full-precision ColBERT MRR@10, improving the footprint-quality tradeoff for memory-constrained late-interaction retrieval.

</details>


### [2] [Quantization Beyond Uniform Bit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19388)
*K. S. Sreeramji,Sabyasachi Basu,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Kirankumar Shiragur,Yujia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可变比特分配框架，通过将嵌入分成连续桶并贪心分配不均匀的存储位数，在固定存储预算下显著提升PQ和SQ的量化质量，尤其适用于具有Matryoshka结构的嵌入，在低比特率下召回率最高提升8%（PQ）和18%（SQ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方案大多与嵌入结构无关，对所有维度均匀分配比特。然而现代模型（如具有Matryoshka属性的MRL嵌入）生成的嵌入具有显著的几何结构，均匀分配在低比特场景下效率低下，因此需要探索依据嵌入结构进行可变比特分配的方案。

Method: 提出一个简单的可变比特分配框架：(1) 将嵌入划分为若干连续桶（bucket）；(2) 采用贪心分配策略，在固定存储预算下对不同的桶非均匀分配比特数；(3) 将该框架分别实例化到乘积量化（PQ）和标量量化（SQ）两种方案中。

Result: 在已知具有Matryoshka属性（MRL）的嵌入上进行实验，非均匀分配在相同存储预算下始终优于均匀分配的基线。最大提升出现在低比特区间，PQ的召回率提升最高达8%，SQ最高达18%。

Conclusion: 可变比特分配为结构感知的压缩与索引技术开辟了新方向，对大规模检索系统的嵌入存储和检索效率具有实用价值。

Abstract: Quantization is a fundamental technique to handle the growing sizes of embeddings generated by modern models. Existing quantization schemes are largely embedding agnostic and allocate bits uniformly across dimensions. However, recent models produce embeddings with significant geometric structure. In this work, we investigate whether a variable bit allocation scheme can improve quantization quality under a fixed memory budget. We propose a simple variable bit allocation framework that partitions an embedding into contiguous buckets and allocates storage non-uniformly across them. Using a greedy allocation strategy, we instantiate this framework for both Product Quantization (PQ) and Scalar Quantization (SQ). We perform a series of experiments on embeddings known to have the Matryoshka property (MRL), and consistently observe that non-uniform allocations outperform uniform baselines at identical storage budgets. The largest improvements occur in the low-bit regime, where uniform allocation is particularly inefficient for MRL embeddings. At the same compression rates, variable allocation improves recall by up to 8\% for PQ and up to 18\% for SQ. Our results suggest a new direction for structure-aware compression and indexing techniques for large-scale retrieval systems.

</details>


### [3] [HARP: Hierarchical Adaptive Ranking with Preference-Adaptive Fusion for Query-Based CVE Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19430)
*Haochen Liu,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Yanchi Liu,Jundong Li,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了HARP框架，一种基于知识图谱的多视角CVE优先级排序方法，通过历史标注案例作为偏好支持库，无需显式文本描述偏好场景，即可从自然语言查询中对漏洞进行排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞优先级排序本质上是偏好依赖的，同一CVE在不同运营偏好场景下应获得不同的修复优先级。现有评分系统和排序方法假设固定标准，而实际组织中偏好往往是隐性的、难以用提示词表达的，历史标注案例比显式偏好描述更容易获取。

Method: HARP框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从漏洞知识图谱中检索证据；(2) 通过策略条件化的全局、企业、用户三个视角对候选CVE进行评分；(3) 从采样支持中拟合视图融合权重，结合自然语言查询与历史标注样本进行排序。

Result: 在三个偏好场景和多种骨干大语言模型上的实验表明，HARP优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了利用历史标注案例作为偏好信号的可行性，提出的多视图图基框架能有效解决隐性偏好场景下的查询式漏洞优先级排序问题。

Abstract: Vulnerability prioritization is inherently preference dependent, since the same CVE can receive different remediation priority under different operational preference scenarios. Existing scoring systems and ranking methods typically assume a fixed criterion. In practice, organizations already operate under a preference scenario, but this preference is often implicit and difficult to express as a written prompt instruction, while triage queries usually do not encode it. Past validated triage cases under the current scenario are more readily available. We study query-based CVE prioritization in this setting and propose HARP, a graph-grounded multi-view framework that ranks candidates from a natural-language query together with a support bank of historical labeled examples from the current preference scenario, without requiring an explicit textual summary of that scenario. HARP retrieves evidence from a vulnerability knowledge graph, scores candidates with policy-conditioned global, enterprise, and user views, and fits view-fusion weights from sampled supports. Experiments across three preference scenarios and multiple backbone LLMs show that HARP outperforms multiple baselines, expressing our method's effectiveness.

</details>


### [4] [SSR-GRPO: Integrating Supervision and Semantic IDs into Reinforcement Learning for Dense Retrieval in E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19595)
*Guangxin Song,Xing Fang,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Bokang Wang,Zhentao Song,Junjie Bai,Jianbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SSR-GRPO是一种用于电子商务搜索的嵌入检索方法，通过双视角相关性评估框架、语义标识符（SIDs）和硬负样本挖掘，解决了R-GRPO在复杂语义理解上的噪声候选与偏差评估问题，已在大型电商平台部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入检索（EBR）在电商搜索中至关重要，但面对复杂和隐式语义时表现不足。现有方法虽然尝试用强化学习（如R-GRPO）提升稠密检索，但存在两个核心问题：(1) 由于批次采样有限导致top-K候选噪声较大；(2) 使用相似训练的LLM作为奖励模型导致相关性评估有偏差。

Method: SSR-GRPO提出三个关键组件：(1) 双视角相关性评估框架：同时利用量化学习生成的语义标识符（SIDs）和稠密表示向量来生成更无偏的相关性得分；(2) 基于SIDs的层次相似关系挖掘硬负样本，并设计掩码函数集成到R-GRPO中过滤组内噪声样本；(3) 构建Retrieval-DPO任务，由正负样本对构成，使模型从成对视角捕获细粒度语义差异。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验验证了SSR-GRPO的有效性，模型已在大型电商平台上实际部署。

Conclusion: SSR-GRPO通过结合语义标识符与稠密表示的双视角评估、基于硬负样本挖掘的掩码机制以及Retrieval-DPO成对优化，有效解决了R-GRPO在复杂语义检索中的噪声与偏差问题，并在真实电商场景中验证了其工业可行性。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is pivotal in e-commerce search but often struggles with complex semantics. While recent methods often fine-tune large language models (LLMs) for representation learning, they typically lack robust mechanisms for handling complex and implicit semantics. While Retrieval-GRPO (R-GRPO) recently introduced reinforcement learning to dense retrieval, it suffers from noisy top-K candidates due to limited batch sampling and biased relevance assessments caused by using similarly trained LLMs as reward models. To tackle these issues, we propose Supervised Retrieval-GRPO with Semantic Identifiers (SSR-GRPO). Specifically, our method first proposes a dual-perspective framework for relevance assessment. It leverages both Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) produced by quantization learning and dense representation vectors to generate more unbiased relevance scores. Furthermore, leveraging the hierarchical similarity relationships of the generated SIDs, we mine a set of hard negative samples that serve two purposes: (1) to design a masking function integrated into R-GRPO, effectively filtering intra-group noisy samples; and (2) to construct a Retrieval-DPO task composed of positive and negative sample pairs, enabling the model to capture fine-grained semantic distinctions from a pair-wise perspective. By integrating these optimization strategies, we propose SSR-GRPO. Extensive offline and online experiments validate SSR-GRPO's effectiveness, and it has been deployed on a large-scale e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [5] [Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19665)
*Kyungho Kim,Sunwoo Kim,Geon Lee,Shinhwan Kang,Sojeong Kim,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Donald Loveland,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoRRe是一个无需训练的推荐框架，采用后LLM范式，将协同过滤信号注入LLM生成的物品表示中，通过物品-物品共购图调整嵌入方向、物品流行度调整嵌入幅度，从而实现比现有训练无关方法和训练方法更具竞争力的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在无需训练的推荐中虽有潜力，但其生成的用户兴趣过于宽泛，难以实现细粒度物品检索。现有方法将协同过滤信号以LLM前处理的方式（如候选重排序或提示增强）引入，收益有限。

Method: 提出CoRRe框架，采用post-LLM范式：首先用LLM生成物品表示，然后将CF信号注入其中——利用物品-物品共购图细化物品嵌入的方向，利用物品流行度调整其幅度，最后将精炼后的物品表示与LLM生成的用户兴趣进行匹配排序。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CoRRe持续优于现有无需训练的方法，并且无需任何模型训练或任务特定微调即可达到与训练方法相比具有竞争力甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CoRRe验证了将CF信号在LLM之后注入的有效性，为训练无关推荐提供了一种新的范式——在不引入训练开销的前提下，通过结构化CF信号精炼LLM生成的表示，显著提升推荐精度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for training-free recommendation, but LLM-generated user interests are often too broad for fine-grained item retrieval. Existing methods incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals in a pre-LLM manner through candidate reranking or prompt augmentation, yielding limited gains. We propose CoRRe, a training-free recommendation framework with a post-LLM paradigm that injects CF signals into LLM-generated item representations, which are later matched with LLM-generated user interests for ranking. Specifically, CoRRe refines the directions of item embeddings using an item-item co-purchase graph and their magnitudes using item popularity. Experiments on real-world datasets show that CoRRe consistently outperforms existing training-free methods and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with training-based methods, without requiring any model training or task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [6] [Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19833)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Praveen Chandar,Paul N. Bennett,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过两个仅依赖近因加权的简单成对探针（SeqRules和PCTM）评估了广泛使用的序列推荐基准，发现在多个基准上这些简单探针的性能优于基于Transformer的eSASRec/SASRec模型，表明现有基准可能无法有效衡量高阶序列建模带来的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管序列推荐器越来越依赖语言模型架构来捕捉复杂的上下文依赖交互，但现有基准是否真正需要这种建模能力尚不明确。作者希望检验广泛使用的基准是否真的能够区分高阶序列建模的能力。

Method: 提出两种简单的不学习高阶序列表示的近因加权成对探针：Sequential Rules (SeqRules) 和 Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM)。使用eSASRec的评估协议，在多个Amazon数据集、MovieLens-1M和MovieLens-20M上对比探针与eSASRec/SASRec的表现。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集上，至少一个探针比eSASRec复现结果高出15-38%，在MovieLens-1M上高出4.4%，但在MovieLens-20M上落后27.3%。在其余四个数据集上，至少一个探针比采样softmax的SASRec复现结果高出9-28%。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的序列推荐基准可能不适合衡量高阶序列建模带来的增益。将Transformer模型与强近因加权成对探针进行比较，可作为一种具体测试手段，用于判断基准是否能够有意义地衡量高阶序列建模的效果。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders increasingly use language-model architectures designed to capture complex, context-dependent interactions. Yet it remains unclear whether widely used benchmarks actually require this modelling capacity. We investigate this question using two simple, recency-weighted pairwise probes that do not learn higher-order sequence representations: Sequential Rules (SeqRules) and our Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM). Using the evaluation protocol of eSASRec, at least one probe exceeds our eSASRec reproduction by 15-38% on three Amazon datasets and by 4.4% on MovieLens-1M, but trails it by 27.3% on MovieLens-20M. On the four remaining datasets, at least one probe also outperforms our sampled-softmax SASRec reproduction by 9-28%, suggesting that these widely used benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring gains from higher-order sequence modelling. More broadly, comparing Transformer-based models against strong recency-weighted pairwise probes provides a concrete test of whether a benchmark can meaningfully measure gains from higher-order sequence modelling.

</details>


### [7] [SCoRD: Semantic-Assisted Continual Retriever-Reranker Distillation for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19998)
*Seunghyun Baek,Gyuseok Lee,Seunghan Lee,Wonbin Kweon,Dong Wang,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCoRD是一个面向LLM重排序流水线的持续知识蒸馏框架，通过语义推理助手将LLM的用户意图推断能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级指导，在非平稳数据流下实现检索器与重排序器的高效协同适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统广泛采用"ID检索器+LLM重排序器"的两阶段流水线，并通过重排序器到检索器的蒸馏来提升检索质量。然而在实际部署中，系统需要不断适应变化的用户兴趣和新交互。简单重复更新LLM重排序器并蒸馏最新知识成本过高，而仅更新检索器成本虽低但受限于稀疏数据难以有效适应。因此需要一个能在非平稳数据流下平衡效率与效果的持续蒸馏框架。

Method: SCoRD包含四个核心机制：(1) 引入语义推理助手，将LLM推断用户潜在意图的能力蒸馏为可复用的意图级引导；(2) 仅在低置信度序列上选择性地将重排序器知识蒸馏给检索器；(3) 在无重复LLM推理的情况下引导检索器单独更新；(4) 将检索器推导的表征和意图漂移信号反馈给重排序器，实现双向协同适应。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SCoRD能够实现高效且有效的检索器-重排序器协同适应。

Conclusion: SCoRD通过选择性蒸馏与双向反馈机制，在降低LLM重排序器重复推理成本的同时，提升了非平稳数据流下检索器与重排序器的联合适应能力，为持续学习场景中的推荐流水线提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recommendation systems increasingly adopt a two-stage pipeline, where an ID-based retriever retrieves candidates and an LLM-based reranker refines their rankings. To improve retrieval quality, reranker-to-retriever distillation is commonly used to transfer the reranker's knowledge to the retriever. For practical deployment, however, this pipeline must continually adapt to evolving interests and incoming interactions. A naive solution is to repeatedly update the LLM reranker and distill its latest knowledge, but this incurs prohibitive costs. Updating the retriever alone is cheaper, but its limited capacity makes adaptation from sparse data difficult. We propose SCoRD, a continual knowledge distillation framework for LLM-based reranking pipelines under a non-stationary data stream. SCoRD introduces a semantic reasoning assistant that distills the LLM's ability to infer underlying user intents into reusable intent-level guidance. It selectively distills reranker knowledge to the retriever on low-confidence sequences, guides retriever-only updates without repeated LLM inference, and feeds retriever-derived representations and intent-drift signals back to the reranker. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SCoRD enables effective and efficient retriever-reranker co-adaptation.

</details>


### [8] [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"自下而上"设计的小语言模型方法：先固定目标（单用户、单token、4-bit权重、普通CPU），再选择合适的架构。模型仅18层中6层保留全注意力，其余12层使用记忆宽度恒为两个时间步的短卷积。在59.9B token上从零训练，以47.31分超过训练前设定的42.20分基线，超越多个训练数据多3-6倍的模型（GPT-2 124M、Pythia-160M等），并超过MobileLLM-125M。相较同规模全注意力模型，混合架构质量提升0.81%，4-bit文件小6.3%，2048 token上下文解码快1.76倍，且速度优势随上下文长度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统做法是先将大语言模型设计好再压缩到CPU上运行，但这种"从上到下"的方式在资源受限场景下效率低下。作者主张反其道而行之：先明确部署目标（单用户、单token推理、4-bit量化、普通CPU），再根据这些约束反向设计架构，以更好地满足边缘端推理需求。

Method: 提出混合架构：18个block中仅6个使用全注意力机制，其余12个使用短卷积层。这些卷积的内存占用仅两个时间步宽，不随对话长度增长而膨胀，因此网络三分之二的层从不重读不断增长的缓存。模型从零开始用59.9B token训练，并进行4-bit权重量化。为验证架构本身的优势，作者同时训练了同等规模的全注意力模型作为对照，并在评分前预先确定获胜条件。

Result: 模型在五任务基准上得分47.31，超过训练前设定的42.20基线；验证bits-per-byte为0.8685。超越GPT-2 124M、Pythia-160M、OPT-125M和GPT-neo-125M（这些模型训练数据多3-6倍），并超过MobileLLM-125M（训练数据达1万亿token）。对照实验中，混合架构质量优于全注意力模型0.81%，4-bit文件小6.3%，2048 token上下文解码快1.76倍（外部同类模型比较为2.08倍）。速度优势在空上下文中接近零，随长度增长，与机制预测一致。简单带宽计算仅预测1.17倍加速，说明内存量不能完全解释差距。

Conclusion: "先定目标再选架构"的设计范式在CPU边缘推理场景下具有显著优势。混合注意力-卷积架构能实现随上下文增长的解码加速，且不牺牲质量。作者同时诚实地报告了失败经验：未缓解的4-bit质量损失、约一半卷积通道失效且无法移除、以及超出该模型规模合理范围的词表大小。

Abstract: Small language models are usually built like large ones and then squeezed onto a CPU afterwards. We did the opposite: we fixed the target first, one user, one token at a time, 4-bit weights, ordinary CPU, and chose the architecture to suit it. The result keeps full attention in only 6 of its 18 blocks. The other 12 use short convolutions whose memory is two timesteps wide no matter how long the conversation gets, so two thirds of the network never re-reads a growing cache.
  Trained from scratch on 59.9B tokens, the model scores 47.31 on a five-task benchmark against a bar of 42.20 that was fixed before training began. It beats GPT-2 124M, Pythia-160M, OPT-125M and GPT-neo-125M, all trained on three to six times more data, and exceeds MobileLLM-125M's published score despite that model seeing a trillion tokens. Validation bits-per-byte is 0.8685.
  To check the architecture rather than the training recipe, we trained a conventional all-attention model of the same size on the same data, and wrote down the winning condition before scoring either. The hybrid won the chosen quality metric by 0.81%, matched it on downstream tasks, produced a 6.3% smaller 4-bit file, and decoded 1.76x faster at 2048 tokens of context, 2.08x against an external model of similar size. In every measurement the speed advantage is near zero at an empty context and grows with length, which is what the mechanism predicts and what a merely leaner model would not show. A simple bandwidth calculation predicts only 1.17x, so memory volume alone does not explain the gap.
  We also report what did not work: an unmitigated 4-bit quality cost, roughly half the convolution channels ending up inert and impossible to remove, and a vocabulary larger than this model size warrants.

</details>


### [9] [What Makes a Good Fiqh Retriever? Answer Retrieval for Arabic Islamic Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20246)
*Somaya Eltanbouly,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz al-Khatib,Shahd Gaben,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对伊斯兰教法问答中的检索增强生成系统，专门研究了阿拉伯语教法学（fiqh）的"含答案检索"问题——即一个段落只有在陈述了问题所要求的具体教法裁决时才被视为相关。作者构建了测试集合，评估了密集、词法、混合、微调及教法学派感知等多种检索策略。最佳检索器MRR@5为0.524，微调后可提升至0.553；混合检索对强模型增益有限，而教法学派感知过滤对学派特定问题使MRR@5提升两倍以上。误差分析表明主要挑战在于区分含答案段落与仅主题相似但不含所需裁决的段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的伊斯兰问答检索增强生成系统大多是端到端评估，无法将检索失败与生成失败分离开来，导致检索环节的问题难以定位和优化。作者因此将研究聚焦于"含答案检索"这一子问题，专门针对阿拉伯语教法学领域构建检索测试集合并评估不同检索策略。

Method: 作者构建了一个阿拉伯语教法学检索测试集合，采用"答案承载"相关性判定标准（段落仅在陈述问题所需裁决时才相关）。在此集合上系统比较了密集检索、词法检索、混合检索、微调模型以及教法学派感知（madhhab-aware）检索策略，并进行误差分析。

Result: 最佳检索器在MRR@5上达到0.524，微调后提升至0.553。混合检索对强模型几乎没有增益；而教法学派感知过滤在学派特定问题上使MRR@5提升超过两倍。误差分析揭示核心挑战是区分"含答案段落"与"仅在主题上相似但不包含所需裁决的段落"。

Conclusion: 检索环节是伊斯兰QA系统性能的关键瓶颈，教法学派感知的过滤机制能显著提升学派特定问题的检索效果。未来工作应聚焦于增强模型对"答案承载性"的判别能力，以更好地区分主题相关与答案相关的段落。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation is used for Islamic question answering, but most systems are evaluated end-to-end, making retrieval failures difficult to isolate from generation failures. We study answer-bearing retrieval for Arabic fiqh, where a passage is relevant only if it states the ruling required by the question. We build a retrieval test collection for Arabic fiqh and use it to evaluate dense, lexical, hybrid, fine-tuned, and madhhab-aware retrieval strategies. The best retriever achieves 0.524 MRR@5, while fine-tuning improves performance to 0.553. Hybrid retrieval provides limited gains for strong models, whereas madhhab-aware filtering more than doubles MRR@5 on school-specific questions. We further present an error analysis showing that the main challenge is distinguishing answer-bearing passages from topically similar passages that do not contain the requested ruling.

</details>


### [10] [Projecting BrowseComp-Plus onto ClimbMix: Toward More Realistic Corpora for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20317)
*Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Yijun Ge,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出BrowseComp-Plus_CM基准测试，通过投影流水线将BrowseComp-Plus的问题证据迁移到ClimbMix（一个400B token、553M文档的预训练web语料库），从而解耦智能体搜索评估与特定检索器。流水线将问题分解为原子推理步骤并在新语料中逐步骤验证，最终从830个问题中筛选出57个完全可验证的问题。投影后将难度转移至检索环节：智能体答案准确率下降5个百分点，证据召回率从84.3%骤降至21.4%，搜索调用增加63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BrowseComp-Plus基准虽然用固定语料替代不透明web搜索以分离智能体与检索器的角色，但其语料仅约10万文档且是从基准自身查询的支持文档和挖掘的困难负样本中构建的，证据和干扰项都按查询专门挑选，存在数据泄漏和过拟合风险。需要一个未参考任何基准构建的大规模语料来更真实地评估智能体搜索能力。

Method: 提出投影流水线（projection pipeline）：将每个问题分解为原子推理步骤，在新语料ClimbMix中为每个步骤寻找证据，仅当自动验证、独立智能体和人工审查三方均确认所有步骤都有支持时才保留该问题。该流水线与数据集无关，适用于任何可分解为可验证事实的基准问题。最终从830个BrowseComp-Plus测试问题中产出57个完全可落地的问题。

Result: 投影后的基准将难度转移至检索环节：最强智能体答案准确率下降5个百分点，证据召回率从84.3%大幅降至21.4%，同时搜索调用次数增加63%。产出了57个完全有证据支撑的问题及问题级相关性判断。

Conclusion: 该投影流水线是系列投影工作的第一步，通过将基准问题迁移到大规模且与基准无关的语料，更公平地评估智能体（agentic）搜索能力。作者发布了流水线、基准和分析代码（github.com/castorini/cmass），供社区使用和扩展。

Abstract: The BrowseComp-Plus benchmark disentangled the evaluation of agentic search by replacing opaque web search with a fixed corpus, so that an agent's role can be separated from the retriever's. That corpus, however, holds only about 100K documents and was assembled from the supporting documents of the benchmark's own queries plus mined hard negatives, so the evidence and the distractors were both selected per query. We introduce $\text{BrowseComp-Plus}_{\text{CM}}$, which keeps the BrowseComp-Plus questions but relocates their evidence to ClimbMix, a 400B-token, 553M-document mixture of web text released by NVIDIA for pre-training language models and built without reference to any benchmark. Our main contribution is the projection pipeline that makes this possible: it decomposes each question into atomic reasoning hops and grounds every hop in the new corpus, retaining a question only when automatic verification, an independent agent, and human review all confirm that every hop is supported. The pipeline is dataset-agnostic and applies to any benchmark whose questions decompose into verifiable facts. Applied to the 830 BrowseComp-Plus test questions, our pipeline yields 57 fully grounded questions with question-level relevance judgments. Projection shifts the difficulty onto retrieval, as the strongest agent we evaluate loses five points of answer accuracy but sees its evidence recall fall from 84.3% to 21.4% while issuing 63% more search calls. As the first of a series of projections, we release the pipeline, the benchmark, and our analyses at https://github.com/castorini/cmass.

</details>

### [11] [The Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP): A Portable Contract for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19263)
*Aniket Wattamwar,Manav Anandani,Mrunal Kakirwar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出评估上下文协议（ECP），一个早期阶段、厂商中立的框架，作为可移植的评估契约层，用于解决AI代理系统评估范式的严重局限性（如基准利用、"自信但错误"现象、理论能力与运营可靠性之间的差距）。ECP通过JSON-RPC接口暴露代理的用户可见输出、工具调用和评估安全的审计上下文，并提供了包含LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI适配器的开源参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能的演进使得评估对象从孤立的LLM转向自主代理架构，而现有评估范式存在严重缺陷：基准利用、代理"自信但错误"的现象，以及理论能力与实际运营可靠性之间的巨大差距。评估基础设施的碎片化问题亟需解决。

Method: 提出ECP（Evaluation Context Protocol）作为厂商中立的可移植评估契约层，定义了一个小型JSON-RPC接口，代理通过该接口暴露用户可见输出、工具调用记录及评估安全的审计上下文；支持跨框架和CI系统运行程序化检查。提供了开源参考实现，包含针对LangChain、LlamaIndex、CrewAI和PydanticAI的适配器。

Result: 论文处于工作进展阶段，尚未完成实证验证。提供了ECP协议的设计方案和开源参考实现，并将设计定位在已有文献记录的失败模式背景中。评估表面、方法集和评分器族预期随协议在更多系统中的实践而演进。

Conclusion: ECP被定位为进行中的工作而非最终标准，其采纳所需的数据验证被列为未来工作。该协议旨在作为代理系统评估的统一契约层，以缓解当前评估基础设施的碎片化问题。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence has necessitated a fundamental shift from evaluating isolated Large Language Models (LLMs) to assessing autonomous agentic architectures. This paper explores the critical methodologies for evaluating AI agents and the essential role of advanced observability infrastructure. We analyze the architectural components of agents and identify the severe limitations of current evaluation paradigms, including benchmark exploitation, the "confidently wrong" phenomenon, and the discrepancy between theoretical capability and operational reliability. To begin addressing the fragmentation in current evaluation infrastructure, this paper proposes the Evaluation Context Protocol (ECP), an early-stage, vendor-neutral framework intended to act as a portable evaluation contract layer for agentic systems. In its current form ECP defines a small JSON-RPC interface over which an agent exposes its user-visible output, the tool calls it made, and evaluator-safe audit context, and against which programmatic checks can be run uniformly across frameworks and continuous integration systems. We describe an open-source reference implementation that includes adapters for LangChain, LlamaIndex, CrewAI, and PydanticAI, and we situate the design against failure modes documented in the recent literature. ECP is presented as work in progress rather than a finished standard: the evaluation surface, method set, and grader families are all expected to change as the protocol is exercised against more systems, and the empirical validation required to justify adoption is outlined as future work.

</details>


### [12] [Stable Within, Unidentified Across: Semantic Identification of Benchmark Effects and Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19269)
*Xi Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估语义识别"(evaluation-semantic identification)的概念，即当评估结论在某个声明的语义族上保持不变时，称其为"工件定义端点Q(s)在评估语义上被识别"。通过TraceElephant系统中的217行冻结分析和因子审计，展示了一个局部非蕴含反例：F_cc可识别T=0，但其并集F_audit无法识别T，说明不同评估者控制的语义选择可导致结论分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估结论往往依赖于评估者控制的语义选择（法律引用、可评分性、聚合方式）。本文旨在揭示这种语义依赖性如何影响结论的确定性，并通过具体案例展示"结论可识别性"在语义族层面上的非传递性。

Method: 本文提出"评估语义识别"的形式化框架：工件定义端点Q(s)在给定语义族上不变量时称其被识别。采用冻结的217行分析数据集，在TraceElephant系统中构建两个对比处理族F_asym和F_cc，并通过因子审计（factorial audit）分解分歧来源。

Result: F_asym产生从-40.76到-43.03个百分点的精确任务分离采纳效应；F_cc中两个联合冻结的可比处理均产生零效应且置信区间为[0,0]；二者并集F_audit无法识别端点T。因子审计将100%的分歧归因于单侧末端删除，非末端贡献为零。

Conclusion: 这是一项局部的非蕴含反例（局部化反例）和可复用的族索引审计，而非流行率估计、穷尽式语义分类学或独立复制研究。结论指出排序结果在对称处理之间仍依赖于具体规格设定。

Abstract: Evaluation conclusions depend on evaluator-controlled semantics: legal references, scoreability, and aggregation. We call an artifact-defined endpoint Q(s) evaluation-semantically identified when it is invariant over a declared family. A frozen 217-row analysis appears stable within its restricted contract family. In TraceElephant, F_asym yields precise task-disjoint adoption effects from -40.76 to -43.03 percentage points, whereas both jointly frozen contrast-comparable treatments in F_cc yield exactly zero with [0, 0] intervals. Their union F_audit therefore does not identify T, even though F_cc identifies T = 0. A factorial audit attributes 100 percent of the disagreement to one-sided terminal deletion, with zero nonterminal contribution. Rankings remain specification-dependent across the symmetric treatments. This is a localized non-implication witness and reusable family-indexed audit, not a prevalence estimate, exhaustive semantic taxonomy, or independent replication.

</details>


### [13] [Hype Meets Reality: Large Language Models as Mutators in Search-based Automated Program Repair of Simulink-Stateflow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19347)
*Ayesha Irshad,Pablo Valle,Jon Ayerdi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在Simulink/Stateflow建模的CPS程序修复中，将流修复（FlowRepair）的变异算子替换为LLM生成的变异，在19个真实故障模型上评估发现：LLM变异显著降低了修复性能（可修复模型从18降至4-6个），揭示了将LLM朴素集成到基于搜索的APR中的根本局限，并建议混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM能否替代基于搜索的APR中精心设计的变异算子，在CPS场景中动态生成更灵活、更具表达力的候选修复。

Method: 扩展FlowRepair方法，将其部分变异算子替换为LLM生成的变异，在19个跨四个CPS领域的真实Stateflow模型上，使用与FlowRepair相同的实验设置和墙钟预算进行受控对比评估。

Result: LLM变异严重降低修复性能：LLM方法仅对4-6个模型产生合理补丁、4个模型产生有效补丁，而原始FlowRepair分别为18个和16个。分析表明LLM在符号化精确编辑上存在困难、缺乏行为反馈、生成噪声搜索空间阻碍有效探索。

Conclusion: 研究不否定LLM在APR中的一般潜力，但揭示朴素集成LLM到搜索式APR存在根本性局限，未来应朝向结合结构化变异与生成式引导的混合方法发展。

Abstract: Search-based Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques rely on carefully designed mutation operators to explore the space of candidate fixes. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that generative models could replace such operators by dynamically proposing repairs. In this paper, we investigate this hypothesis in the context of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) modeled in Simulink/Stateflow. We extend the state-of-the-art FlowRepair approach by replacing a subset of its mutation operators with LLM-generated mutations, enabling more flexible and expressive patch generation. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark of 19 real-world faulty Stateflow models across four CPS domains, using the same experimental setup as FlowRepair for controlled comparison under the same wall-clock budget. Contrary to expectations, in this controlled evaluation, the LLM-based mutation substantially degrades repair performance under the FlowRepair experimental setup. Across the tested LLM variants, the LLM-based repair produced plausible patches for 4-6 models and valid patches for 4 models, compared to 18 and 16, respectively, with the original approach. Our analysis reveals that, in this integration, LLMs struggle with precise symbolic edits, lack behavioral feedback, and generate a noisy search space that hinders effective exploration. Rather than showing a general limitation of LLMs for APR, these findings highlight fundamental limitations of naively integrating LLMs into search-based APR and motivate hybrid approaches that combine structured mutation with generative guidance.

</details>


### [14] [RD-Gen: Random DAG Generator Considering Multi-rate Applications for Reproducible Scheduling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19460)
*Atsushi Yano,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RD-Gen，一个考虑多速率应用的随机DAG生成器，用于可复现的实时系统调度评估，支持批量生成具有不同参数的随机DAG集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统日益复杂，DAG模型被广泛用于表达任务依赖和并行性，且需要随机DAG集来验证方法的有效性和客观性。然而现有工具无法生成考虑最新多速率应用的随机DAG集，研究者需自行生成，导致额外工作量、可靠性低且难以复现。

Method: 提出RD-Gen随机DAG生成器，专门针对多速率应用场景设计，支持批量生成具有不同参数的随机DAG集，满足实时系统调度评估的可复现性需求。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了RD-Gen能够管理各种问题设置和DAG研究需求，展示了其在多速率应用场景下的适用性。

Conclusion: RD-Gen解决了现有随机DAG生成工具缺乏多速率应用支持的问题，提升了实时系统调度研究的可靠性和可复现性，为研究者提供了标准化、可批量的DAG生成能力。

Abstract: Real-time systems have various requirements such as the deadline and resource constraints. In addition, real-time systems are becoming larger and more complex, and studies on performance analysis and efficient scheduling algorithms are becoming increasingly important. Directed acyclic graph (DAG) models, which can express task dependencies and parallelism, are used for such studies. Random DAG sets are used to demonstrate the effectiveness and objectivity of methods proposed for real-time systems. However, there is no random DAG generation tool available that can generate a DAG set that considers the latest multi-rate applications. Therefore, researchers need to generate random DAG sets on their own, leading to additional effort and reduced reliability and reproducibility. To solve this problem, we propose a random DAG generator considering multi-rate applications for reproducible scheduling evaluation (RD-Gen). RD-Gen also enables batch generation of random DAG sets with different parameters. Case studies are used to demonstrate that RD-Gen can manage various problem settings and DAG study requirements.

</details>


### [15] [Measuring What a Specification Determines: A Formal Semantic-Block Model and an Execution-Judged Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19475)
*Oleg Grynets,Dmytro Kostetskyi,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的语义块模型（semantic-block model）用于规格说明（specification），并构建了一个以执行为判据的基准来独立于模型能力评估规格质量。该模型由语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和显式开放问题组成，并受四个机器可检查的良构性条件约束。确定性（determinacy）被模型论定义为所有一致实现之间的共识，并通过独立实现者的收敛性进行经验估计。在Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移规格上实例化后，计算验证显示该五层分解使平均每任务上下文减少约71%，覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类体系，且未被备选分区帕累托支配。基准固定实现者面板，包含必做的无规格对照臂，以PostgreSQL 16和实时Oracle实例作为确定性执行裁判。六项设计研究表明，确定性作为一个形式化概念是成立的，但不能作为评估当代LLM实现者的独立经验质量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规格说明（specification）的质量评估往往与模型能力耦合，难以独立判断规格本身的好坏。作者旨在提出一个不依赖具体实现模型能力的规格质量评估框架，通过形式化语义块模型和基于执行的基准，将规格质量与模型能力解耦，并探究确定性（determinacy）是否能作为独立的经验质量指标。

Method: 提出形式化语义块模型，将规格表示为语义块、依赖关系、块属规则、决策点和开放问题的组合，并定义四个机器可检查的良构性条件（无环性、单一归属、约束支配、完全性或歧义停止）。构建执行裁判基准，固定实现者面板，设无规格对照臂，以PostgreSQL 16和实时Oracle实例作为确定性执行裁判。通过多实现者收敛性估计确定性，并在Oracle-to-PostgreSQL迁移规格（18块、19条依赖边）上实例化验证。设计六个研究（含三个预注册操纵和三个诊断分析），并对25单元子样本进行重复运行以测量经验变异下限。

Result: 计算验证表明：五层分解使平均每任务上下文减少约71%（通过依赖闭包），覆盖85.5%的Oracle构造分类体系且所有缺口均被分类处理，该分解未被测试的备选分区帕累托支配，且在99.9分位点上能从非用于定义原结构的引文派生边中恢复。重复运行显示经验变异下限的中位数臂差分布为14.4个百分点。六项研究的结论支持确定性作为形式化概念，但不支持其作为评估当代LLM实现者的独立经验质量指标。

Conclusion: 确定性在模型论上是一个可严格定义且有价值的形式化概念，但从经验角度看，在当前LLM实现者群体中，它不能单独作为规格质量的可靠度量标准。这提示需要开发更细粒度或多维度的规格质量评估方法，而不能仅凭确定性来判定规格优劣。

Abstract: This work introduces a formal semantic-block model for specifications and an execution-judged benchmark for evaluating specification quality independently of model capability. A specification is represented as a structure comprising semantic blocks, dependency relations, block-owned rules, decision points, and explicitly open questions, subject to four machine-checkable well-formedness conditions: acyclicity, single ownership, constraint domination, and totality or ambiguity-stop. Determinacy is defined model-theoretically as agreement among all conforming implementations and is estimated empirically through convergence across independent implementers. The model is instantiated on an Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration specification containing 18 blocks and 19 dependency edges. Computational validation shows that the five-layer decomposition reduces mean per-task context by approximately 71% through dependency closures, covers 85.5% of the study-defined Oracle construct taxonomy with all identified gaps triaged, is not Pareto-dominated by the tested alternative partitions, and is recovered at the 99.9th percentile from citation-derived edges not used to define the original structure. The benchmark keeps the implementer panel fixed, includes a mandatory no-specification control arm, and uses PostgreSQL 16 and a live Oracle instance as deterministic execution judges. Six designed studies, including three pre-registered manipulations and three diagnostic analyses, further examine specification effects. Repeated runs on a 25-unit subsample reveal an empirical variability floor with a median arm-delta spread of 14.4 percentage points. The results support determinacy as a formal concept but not as a standalone empirical quality metric for the evaluated contemporary LLM implementers.

</details>


### [16] [Accelerated Genetic Programming Hyper-Heuristics for Simulation-Based Scheduling via Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19487)
*Heyang Thomas Li,Alexander Pletzer,Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用Claude代理AI对Python科研代码进行系统性重构的方法，针对离散事件项目调度仿真中的性能瓶颈。在高性能计算环境中，该方法将测试运行时间从1,298秒降至200秒以下，且输出不变，每年节省400万核心小时（相当于32万新西兰元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python在科研中被广泛使用，但定制化研究代码在实验规模扩大时可能变得异常缓慢。离散事件项目调度仿真尤为突出，因为顺序状态更新、嵌套循环、条件评估和面向对象结构限制了编译数值库和GPU加速库的优势。传统优化需要反复的性能分析、重构、测试和验证，而研究人员往往缺乏时间或专门的软件工程专业知识来进行底层优化。

Method: 采用Claude代理AI在HPC环境中对真实项目调度工作负载进行系统性重构。代理以代表性基准测试和正确性检查为引导，识别瓶颈、实施针对性优化并评估其效果。研究者在整个过程中保留最终控制权。

Result: 测试运行时间从1,298秒减少到200秒以下，且输出保持不变；每年节省了400万核心小时的计算资源（价值32万新西兰元）。

Conclusion: Claude代理AI结合正确性检查的重构方法能够有效优化Python科研代码的性能瓶颈，在保证输出正确性的前提下实现显著的性能提升和资源节约，为科研人员在缺乏专业软件工程技能的情况下提供了一种高效的代码优化途径。

Abstract: Python is widely used in scientific research because it enables rapid development and provides rich ecosystems for data analysis, artificial intelligence (AI), and machine learning. However, customized research code can become prohibitively slow as experiments scale. This challenge is particularly acute in discrete-event project-scheduling simulations, where sequential state updates, nested loops, conditional evaluations, and object-oriented structures limit the benefits of compiled numerical and GPU-accelerated libraries. Addressing these bottlenecks typically requires iterative profiling, refactoring, testing, and validation, yet researchers may lack the time or specialized software-engineering expertise for low-level optimization. This paper presents a systematic refactoring approach using Claude agentic AI on real-world project-scheduling workloads in a high-performance computing (HPC) environment. Guided by representative benchmarks and correctness checks, the agent identifies bottlenecks, implements targeted optimizations, and evaluates their effects, while the researcher retains final control. Testing runtime reduced from 1,298 seconds to under 200 seconds without changing outputs, saving four million core-hours (NZ\$320,000) annually.

</details>


### [17] [An Agentic RAG and Evaluation Framework for Assurance Case Generation: Industrial Use Case for the EU Cyber Resilience Act Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19509)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Iker Lasa Ojanguren,Dusica Marijan,Maria I. Maslioukova,José Arias Marin,Pavlos Kosmides*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG）的自动化框架，用于生成符合欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）要求的保证案例（Assurance Cases），通过形式化的Claim-Argument-Evidence（主张-论证-证据）逻辑，将技术文档要求系统性地映射为认证证据，并在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统上进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业（SMEs）在遵守欧盟《网络弹性法案》（CRA）时面临资源密集型的挑战，因为网络安全合规性评估过程复杂。合规对于证明监管合规性及确保产品安全性和弹性至关重要，但传统的手动合规评估方法对资源有限的中小企业负担沉重。

Method: 该框架采用基于形式化Claim-Argument-Evidence逻辑的智能体检索增强生成（agentic RAG）技术，通过系统性地映射技术文档要求，自动生成保证案例（ACs），并引入自然语言推理（NLI）评估器来验证证据的grounding与可追溯性。

Result: 在Catalink的PATROLIoT野火监测系统案例研究中，该框架生成了70个保证案例，具有高grounding密度（每个AC约4.4个文档工件）；NLI评估器达到0.88的准确率；专家验证的可信度评分为3.06，支持可解释的论证。

Conclusion: 该工作为从业人员提供了一种可扩展、可解释的自动化方法，用于执行强制性的CRA合规评估，在减少人工工作量的同时保持透明的决策支持，有助于缓解中小企业面临的资源压力。

Abstract: Complying with the EU Cyber Resilience Act (CRA) is a resource-intensive challenge for SMEs due to the complexity of cybersecurity conformity assessment. Yet, it is essential for demonstrating regulatory compliance and ensuring product security and resilience. To address this, we introduce an automated framework for generating Assurance Cases (ACs) using an agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounded in a formal Claim-Argument-Evidence logic. By systematically mapping technical documentation requirements, the framework streamlines the generation of certification evidence. We validate our approach on a case study of Catalink's PATROLIoT wildfire monitoring system, where the agentic RAG generated 70 ACs with high grounding density (~4.4 artefacts per AC). The proposed Natural Language Inference (NLI) evaluator achieves 0.88 accuracy, which provides robust evidence grounding and traceability, while expert-validated plausibility (3.06) supports interpretable justifications. For practitioners, this work provides a scalable, interpretable approach for automating mandatory CRA conformity assessments, reducing manual effort while maintaining transparent decision support.

</details>


### [18] [Trace-Based Execution-Level Observability of VDM-SL Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19510)
*Tomohiro Oda,Han-Myung Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出在VDM规范执行过程中记录赋值、操作调用和返回语句的执行轨迹，将这些内部行为持久化为基于状态的模型，并在ViennaTalk中实现事件数据模型，用于可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM虽然通过定理证明和软件测试进行验证，但动画执行和调试器仅能提供即时观察，操作内部的执行行为缺乏持久化和可分析性，难以系统地追溯和研究操作执行过程。

Method: 记录执行轨迹中的事件（赋值、操作调用、返回语句），建立基于状态的事件数据模型，在ViennaTalk工具中实现该模型，并将其应用于可视化展示。

Result: 提出了执行轨迹事件的数据模型及其在ViennaTalk中的实现，并将其应用于操作内部行为的可视化分析。

Conclusion: 通过记录和利用执行轨迹，使VDM操作的内部行为变得可持久化和可分析，为规范验证提供了新的分析维度。

Abstract: VDM has been pursuing rigorous verification through mathematical theorem proving and software testing via simulated execution. Animation through an interpreter enables validation of the specification to ensure it meets the required functionality. Step-by-step execution in a debugger also allows the user to follow the internal behavior of operations. In this paper, we propose the recording and utilization of execution traces of assignments, operation calls, and return statements to make the internal behavior of operations persistent and analyzable as state-based models. The data model of events in execution traces, its implementation in ViennaTalk, and its application to visualization will be introduced.

</details>


### [19] [Garbage Collection and Energy Consumption in Java: A Controlled Study Across Workloads and JDKs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19520)
*Rahil Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对三种Java垃圾收集器（Serial、Parallel和G1）在不同应用程序、工作负载强度和JDK发行版下进行了受控实证评估，发现收集器之间不存在统计上可靠的能耗差异，而工作负载强度才是能耗的显著驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垃圾收集器选择是一个低成本配置决策，但能显著影响Java应用的性能和能耗，然而现有研究不清楚聚合能耗排名能否在不同应用、工作负载强度和JDK发行版间泛化，因此需要系统的实证研究来澄清这一不确定性。

Method: 采用受控实证评估方法：跨三个Java应用、三种工作负载强度和两个JDK发行版（OpenJDK和Oracle JDK）测试Serial、Parallel和G1收集器；使用EnergiBridge测量处理器封装能耗和执行时间，并推导补充的能耗-性能指标；采用RCBD（随机完全区组设计）ANOVA进行统计分析。

Result: Parallel记录最低平均能耗（839.8 J），Serial次之（857.6 J），G1最高（969.0 J）；但RCBD ANOVA未建立统计显著的收集器效应。工作负载强度是能耗的显著驱动因素，重负载显著消耗更多能量。能耗与执行时间呈中度正相关（r=0.33）。OpenJDK与Oracle JDK之间无显著能耗差异。

Conclusion: 研究不支持存在通用最优能耗的垃圾收集器；工作负载强度是管理Java能耗更可靠的杠杆；垃圾收集器选择应作为应用特定的调优决策，基于目标系统上的实际测量而非聚合排名。

Abstract: Garbage-collector selection is a low-effort configuration decision that can influence both the performance and energy consumption of Java applications. However, it remains unclear whether aggregate energy rankings among collectors generalise across heterogeneous applications, workload intensities, and JDK distributions. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of Serial, Parallel, and G1 garbage collection across three Java applications, three workload intensities, and two JDK distributions: OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Using EnergiBridge, processor-package energy consumption and execution time were measured and complementary energy-performance metrics derived. Across all evaluated configurations, Parallel recorded the lowest mean energy consumption (839.8 J), followed closely by Serial (857.6 J) and G1 (969.0 J), but an RCBD ANOVA did not establish a statistically reliable collector effect. Workload intensity, by contrast, was a significant driver of energy consumption, with heavy workloads consuming substantially more energy than light or medium workloads regardless of collector. Energy consumption showed a moderate positive association with execution time (r = 0.33), indicating that longer-running configurations tended toward higher energy use, though the relationship was far from proportional. No statistically significant energy difference was found between OpenJDK and Oracle JDK. Overall, the results do not support a universally energy-optimal garbage collector; instead, workload intensity emerges as the more reliable lever for managing Java energy consumption, and collector selection should be treated as an application-specific tuning decision supported by measurement on the target system rather than aggregate rankings alone.

</details>


### [20] [Auditing and Decomposing Feedback-Driven Evolution in LLM Test Generation under the Oracle Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19626)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了反馈驱动测试生成中"执行反馈作为自验证信号"的可靠性问题。研究发现，以单个已接受程序的输出作为ground truth会产生虚假的故障检测和虚假的演化收益（过度膨胀9.46-14.85个百分点）。经过审计后，等预算独立重采样反而优于基于突变的演化。此外，通过密度匹配的安慰剂对照实验，真实反馈循环并未提供稳健的细粒度反馈收益。作者提出了audit-and-placebo协议来分离验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈信用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM测试生成方法常将执行反馈视为自验证信号来改进生成的测试，但当生成输入在单一已接受程序上执行并将其输出作为ground truth时，无效或不明确的输入会产生虚假的故障检测和看似合理的演化收益。作者旨在系统审计这一失败模式，以揭示反馈驱动测试生成中真实收益与伪影之间的混淆。

Method: 使用142个开发任务、114个锁定的外部任务和138个held-out任务，结合两个代码模型、三个随机种子和fault-cross-fitted的真实提交。对三个已接受实现达成一致的输入，比较生成输出与panel的一致性。对比单参考oracle与panel-oracle的收益差异，用等预算独立重采样与突变演化对比，设计三轮回圈反馈与密度匹配安慰剂的对照实验，并由两名软件工程博士生进行盲法语义审计。

Result: 外部输入中生成输出与panel一致率仅为27.79%和50.12%；单参考oracle使演化收益膨胀9.46-14.85个百分点；审计后等预算独立重采样优于基于突变的演化6.01-18.83个百分点。真实反馈与安慰剂在外部数据上的差异为+0.13和-0.50点，held-out数据为+1.99和+0.28点，无稳健反馈收益证据。盲法审计分类94.41%的panel不一致输入为无效，但3.60%为有效。

Conclusion: 单参考执行反馈作为ground truth不可靠，会显著夸大演化收益；panel不一致虽然提供信息但不能作为语义证明。未来的自演化测试生成器评估应采用audit-and-placebo协议，以区分验证器伪影、交互脚手架和真实反馈的贡献。

Abstract: Execution feedback is often treated as a self-verifying signal for improving LLM-generated tests. However, when generated inputs are executed on a single accepted program and its outputs are used as ground truth, invalid or underspecified inputs can create spurious fault detections and apparent evolutionary gains. We audit this failure mode in feedback-driven test generation using 142 development tasks, 114 locked external tasks, and 138 held-out tasks, with two code models, three seeds, and fault-cross-fitted real submissions. On external inputs for which three accepted implementations agree, generated outputs match the panel on only 27.79% and 50.12% of cases. A single-reference oracle inflates the measured gain from evolution by 9.46-14.85 percentage points; after auditing, equal-budget independent resampling outperforms mutation-based evolution by 6.01-18.83 points. We further compare a genuine three-round feedback loop with a density-matched placebo. External Real-Placebo differences are +0.13 and -0.50 points, while held-out differences are +1.99 and +0.28 points and do not provide robust evidence of fine-grained feedback benefit. A blinded semantic audit by two software engineering doctoral students classifies 94.41% of panel-disconfirmed inputs as invalid but 3.60% as valid, showing that panel disagreement is informative but not semantic proof. We propose an audit-and-placebo protocol that separates verifier artifacts, interaction scaffolding, and grounded feedback credit in evaluations of self-evolving test generators.

</details>


### [21] [Loreley: Repository-Scale Program Evolution with Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19703)
*Mohan Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Loreley，一种在质量-多样性（QD）档案中保留完整仓库状态并作为编辑父代或上下文的智能体搜索方法。在匹配的Zstandard对照实验中（1,008个物理任务），QD在48个任务时的性能比Sequential Champion低0.135%（95% BCa置信区间-0.556%至+0.161%），比Independent Root高0.320%（区间-0.082%至+0.686%）；两个对比均未确立QD优势，Sequential获得最高的48任务均值和中位数。QD确实发生了档案保留和后续采样，四至六个最终获胜者包含非在位父代祖先，但对照实验未显示端到端收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列智能体搜索只保留当前最优解而丢弃替代分支，存在局部收敛风险；独立并行提案虽保持探索广度，但每次都从根节点重新开始，缺乏累积。需要一种既能保留搜索历史多样性又能复用中间状态的机制，以提升代码优化等领域的搜索效率。

Method: Loreley将完整的仓库状态（Git提交）存入质量-多样性（QD）档案，从中采样作为后续编辑的父代或上下文。候选通过隔离的worktree生成，由项目提供的评估器评判。在Zstandard实验中设计了七组配对块，每策略每块48个物理候选任务（共1,008个），根初始化并采用各策略原生并发。使用验证集在多个预算节点选择获胜者，隐藏holdout集评估固定候选。

Result: 在48个任务节点：QD比Sequential Champion低0.135%（区间-0.556%至+0.161%），比Independent Root高0.320%（区间-0.082%至+0.686%），两个对比均未达到统计显著优势；Sequential的均值和均值都最高。QD的档案保留和后续采样确实发生：四项最终QD获胜者的主父代谱系包含非在位状态（回顾性单在位规则），加入灵感边后增至六项。早期三个能力活动为两个Python库和独立Zstandard修订产生了第4代多文件改进。

Conclusion: Loreley成功触发了踏板石机制（stepping-stone mechanism），档案保留和采样行为得到验证，但受控实验中未显示48个任务下的端点收益。QD策略在给定预算内不优于顺序冠军编辑，需要更大预算或改进采样策略来验证其潜在价值。

Abstract: Sequential agent search accumulates changes from its current champion but discards alternative branches; independent proposals preserve breadth but restart from the root. Loreley instead retains complete repository states in a Quality-Diversity (QD) archive and samples them as parents or supplies them as context for later edits. Candidates are Git commits produced in isolated worktrees and judged by a project-supplied evaluator. We compare configured Loreley QD, sequential champion editing, and independent root proposals in a matched Zstandard experiment: seven paired blocks and 48 physical candidate jobs per policy and block (1,008 total), with root-only initialization and each policy's native concurrency. Validation selected a winner at each budget checkpoint; an agent-hidden holdout measured the fixed candidate. At 48 jobs, QD was 0.135% below Sequential Champion (95% BCa interval for the paired effect: -0.556% to +0.161%) and 0.320% above Independent Root (-0.082% to +0.686%). Neither contrast established a QD advantage; Sequential had the highest observed 48-job mean and median. Archive retention and later sampling did occur. Four of seven final QD winners had a non-incumbent state in their primary-parent ancestry under a retrospective one-incumbent rule applied only to the observed QD stream. Including inspiration edges raised the count to six, without showing that supplied context caused an edit. Three earlier capability campaigns produced generation-4, multi-file improvements in two Python libraries and a separate Zstandard revision. Loreley engaged the intended stepping-stone mechanism, but the controlled experiment did not show an endpoint benefit at 48 jobs.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向IoT汽车应用的端到端、部署感知测试流水线，结合LLM/VLM辅助的测试与代码生成、人工参与审核，并利用Eclipse openDuT支持跨地域分布式测试执行。通过儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）案例验证，实现了9条需求100%功能覆盖和Gherkin生成100%准确率。

Result: 以儿童存在检测系统（CPDS）为案例，实现了全部9条需求的完整功能覆盖，受控需求集上Gherkin生成准确率达100%；通过Eclipse openDuT在跨地理分布的ECU上进行分布式测试执行，验证了该流水线在OEM-供应商测试工作流中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该部署感知测试流水线有效结合了生成式AI与分布式执行框架，能够减少人工测试负担、提升一致性和覆盖度，适用于OEM与供应商之间的跨组织汽车软件测试场景。

Abstract: Testing embedded software in modern vehicles is challenging due to system complexity, decentralized architectures, and strict safety and performance constraints. In this work, we present an end-to-end, deployment-aware testing pipeline for IoT-based automotive applications. The pipeline combines requirement-driven test and code generation with large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) assistance, and human-in-the-loop curation to reduce manual effort and improve consistency. Using Eclipse openDuT, it supports flexible, distributed deployment across geographically separated cyber-physical and IoT infrastructures, optimizing for node availability and cross-organizational coordination. For validation, we conduct a case study using a Child Presence Detection System (CPDS), achieving full functional requirement coverage across all 9 requirements and 100% Gherkin generation accuracy on the controlled requirement set. Distributed test execution across geographically separated ECUs via Eclipse openDuT confirms the pipeline's applicability to OEM--supplier testing workflows.

</details>


### [23] [PRAXIS: Graph-Grounded Tacit Knowledge for Domain Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19784)
*Xue Jiang,Tianyu Zhang,Lingwei Wu,Ziyu Wang,Ge Li,Yuan Sui,Hao Zhu,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAXIS框架，通过模拟人类开发流程从代码库中系统性地提取、表示和复用隐性知识（tacit knowledge），以解决LLM智能体在领域特定代码生成任务上表现不佳的问题。实验表明PRAXIS优于现有最强智能体及经验/技能学习方法，并能无缝集成到多种智能体框架与LLM中，且支持随实践积累持续进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在通用软件工程任务上表现优异，但在领域特定代码生成上存在瓶颈。作者识别出根本原因在于智能体缺乏隐性知识——包括领域业务规则、接口契约和操作约定，这些知识被开发者通过实践内化但从未记录在文档中。这类知识深埋于领域代码之下，分散在代码实体及其依赖关系中，难以检索和学习。

Method: PRAXIS框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 通过模拟人类开发工作流程在目标代码库中获取隐性知识；(2) 将获取的知识蒸馏为结构化单元，并组织在代码依赖图上；(3) 在代码交互的关键节点主动将相关知识呈现给智能体，实现知识的按需复用。

Result: 大量实验表明PRAXIS在领域代码生成上优于配备强大智能搜索能力的最新SOTA智能体，也优于基于经验和基于技能的方法。该框架能无缝集成到多种智能体框架和不同LLM中，保持一致的性能提升；同时支持持续进化，随着实践积累增加性能稳步提升。

Conclusion: PRAXIS有效解决了LLM智能体在领域代码生成中缺乏隐性知识的核心问题，通过将隐性知识显式地提取、结构化表示并按需复用，显著提升了领域代码生成性能，且具有良好的通用性、兼容性和可扩展性。

Abstract: LLM agents have achieved strong performance on general software engineering tasks, yet struggle with domain-specific code generation. We identify the root cause as the agent's lack of tacit knowledge, including domain-specific business rules, interface contracts, and operational conventions that developers internalize through practice but never document. This knowledge is deeply buried beneath the domain code, dispersed across code entities and their dependency relations, and invisible to the agent that lacks it. These properties make tacit knowledge inherently difficult to retrieve or learn. In this work, we propose PRAXIS, a framework that enables agents to systematically extract, represent, and reuse tacit knowledge for domain code generation. PRAXIS acquires tacit knowledge by simulating human development workflows within the target codebase, distills it into structured units organized on the code dependency graph, and proactively surfaces it to the agent at the point of code interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PRAXIS outperforms state-of-the-art agents equipped with powerful agentic search capabilities, as well as experience-based and skill-based methods. The approach integrates seamlessly into various agent frameworks and LLMs with consistent performance improvements, and supports continual evolution with performance steadily scaling as practice accumulates.

</details>


### [24] [Further Progress Towards Operation Proof Obligation Generation for VDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19848)
*Nick Battle,Carlo Rende,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对VDM形式化方法中证明义务生成工作的更新，改进了循环不变量、增加了循环变体、改进了操作调用的证明义务（包括隐式声明操作和规范语句），并增加了操作的递归度量，通过示例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VDM形式化方法需要通过称为证明义务的断言来确保模型内部的一致性，之前的证明义务生成工作存在不足，需要更新和改进。

Method: 对VDM操作的证明义务生成方法进行了多项改进：改进循环不变量的处理方式、新增循环变体、改进操作调用的证明义务（涵盖隐式声明操作和规范语句）、增加操作的递归度量。

Result: 通过示例展示了各项新功能的具体应用效果，涵盖了循环不变量、循环变体、操作调用（含隐式操作和规范语句）以及递归度量等方面的改进。

Conclusion: 该工作是对文献[1]所述VDM证明义务生成方法的更新，提供了更完善的证明义务生成机制，增强了VDM模型验证的能力。

Abstract: The VDM formalism can ensure that models are internally consistent. Potential inconsistencies are highlighted by assertions called proof obligations. This paper is an update to the work described in [1], regarding proof obligation generation for VDM operations. We present the latest work which improves the approach to loop invariants, adds loop variants, improves proof obligations for operation calls, including implicitly declared operations and specification statements, and adds recursive measures for operations. The new features are illustrated with examples.

</details>


### [25] [Repo0: Design-Driven Zero-to-All Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19854)
*Silin Chen,Haoyi Teng,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Jiale Huang,Yongpan Wang,Hongyu Zhang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Repo0是一个面向"从零到全"代码生成的连续结构演化框架，通过双DAG（需求层DAG、组件层DAG及其对齐关系）维护显式架构状态，迭代演化组件边界直至结构收敛，再以收敛架构指导测试驱动代码生成。在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上，使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2评估，Repo0在功能覆盖率和通过率上均达最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在代码生成时大多假设预定义的仓库架构，但这一假设在从自然语言需求直接构建完整软件项目（zero-to-all代码生成）的场景中不成立。该场景要求智能体在开发全程维护模块化的仓库架构，而现有方法缺乏对架构状态的显式建模和持续演化机制。

Method: Repo0维护一个显式的架构状态——双有向无环图（Dual-DAG），由需求层DAG、组件层DAG及二者间的对齐关系组成。框架从自然语言需求出发，通过结构动作（structural actions）迭代演化组件边界，并以模块化度量（modularity metrics）作为指导信号，持续进行直至结构收敛；收敛后的架构随后引导测试驱动开发（TDD）的代码生成过程。

Result: 在RepoCraft的六个真实仓库上使用GPT-5 mini和DeepSeek V3.2评估，Repo0在所有设置下均取得最高的功能覆盖率（Functionality Coverage）和通过率（Pass Rate）。相比最强的仓库规划基线RPG，功能覆盖率最高提升20.08个百分点，通过率最高提升29.74个百分点。消融实验和结构演化分析验证了Dual-DAG架构状态、模块化引导的结构演化以及显式结构收敛的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式建模并持续演化架构状态对zero-to-all代码生成任务至关重要。Repo0通过Dual-DAG架构表示、模块化度量引导的结构演化和显式结构收敛机制，有效解决了缺乏预定义架构时大规模代码生成的结构质量问题，为这一领域提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: Large language model agents have made substantial progress in code generation, yet most existing systems assume a predefined repository architecture. This assumption does not hold in zero-to-all code generation, where an agent must construct an entire software project directly from natural-language requirements while maintaining a modular repository architecture throughout development. We present Repo0, a continuous structural evolution framework for zero-to-all code generation. Repo0 maintains an explicit architectural state instantiated as a Dual-Directed-Acyclic-Graph (Dual-DAG), consisting of a requirement-level DAG, a component-level DAG, and their alignment relation. Starting from natural-language requirements, it iteratively evolves component boundaries through structural actions guided by modularity metrics until structural convergence, after which the converged architecture guides test-driven development code generation. We evaluate Repo0 on six real-world repositories from RepoCraft using GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek V3.2. Repo0 achieves the highest Functionality Coverage and Pass Rate across all settings. Compared with RPG, the strongest repository-planning baseline, Repo0 improves Functionality Coverage by up to 20.08 percentage points and Pass Rate by up to 29.74 percentage points. Ablation and structural-evolution analyses further demonstrate the importance of the Dual-DAG architectural state, modularity-guided structural evolution, and explicit structural convergence.

</details>


### [26] [Evaluating Automated Testing on an Open-Source Web Application Using Cypress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19960)
*Quoc-Binh Nguyen,Truc-Ly Phan Nguyen,Ngoc Hong Tran,Dung Hai Dinh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了使用Cypress框架对开源Web应用进行端到端自动化测试的有效性，实验部署了27个测试用例，从执行速度、可靠性和可维护性三个维度衡量测试效果。结果表明Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合开发过程中的频繁运行；大多数测试用例在多次运行中结果一致，仅在涉及复杂交互功能的少数测试中出现flakiness；此外，使用data-cy属性和Page Object Model架构可显著降低UI变更时的维护开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动化测试在Web软件开发中被广泛使用，但其真实有效性受执行时间、结果稳定性和测试套件可维护性等多因素影响，需要系统地评估这些因素以指导实践。

Method: 在开源Web应用上部署基于Cypress框架的端到端自动化测试实验，设计了27个测试用例，从执行速度、可靠性（多轮运行结果一致性）和维护性（元素定位策略与POM架构的影响）三个维度进行量化评估。

Result: 实验结果显示：Cypress测试套件执行时间短且稳定，适合开发过程中的频繁运行；绝大多数测试用例在多次运行中结果一致；只有涉及复杂交互的少数测试出现flakiness；data-cy属性和POM架构的设计能显著降低UI变更时的维护开销。

Conclusion: 研究验证了Cypress框架在端到端测试中的有效性，同时揭示了测试套件的可维护性高度依赖元素定位策略与架构设计，采用弹性好的data-cy属性和POM模式是降低维护成本的关键。

Abstract: End-to-end automated testing is increasingly used in web software development to ensure system quality and shorten response times during development. However, the true effectiveness of automated testing depends on many factors including execution time, stability of test results, and maintainability of the test suite as the application continues to evolve. In this paper, we evaluate the effectiveness of end-to-end automated testing using the Cypress framework for an open-source web application. We deployed the experiment with 27 test cases. The test's effectiveness is measured by execution speed, reliability, and maintainability. The experimental results show that the Cypress-based end-to-end test suite has short and stable execution times. It suits frequent runs during software development. The majority of test cases achieved consistent results across multiple runs, while flakiness only occurred in a few tests which involve complex interactive functions. Furthermore, the study highlights the impact of element locator strategies and Page Object Model (POM) architecture on test suite maintainability, demonstrating that resilient data-cy attributes significantly reduce maintenance overhead when UI changes occur.

</details>


### [27] [BreakGuard: Towards Detecting Dependency Breaking Changes with LLM-Generated Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20167)
*Rachna Raj,Benoit Baudry,Diego Elias Costa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BreakGuard是一种利用大语言模型（LLM）生成测试套件来检测客户端库升级中破坏性变更（BCs）的方法。它静态提取调用目标库方法的客户端焦点方法，为每个方法生成测试，通过在旧版本上通过、新版本上失败来识别BC。在89个真实BC上，最佳配置检测到30.3%的BC，平均每个检出成本约0.90美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在演进过程中会引入破坏性变更（BCs），破坏与客户端建立的契约并导致客户端应用故障。然而客户端侧测试套件由于库覆盖率有限，往往无法检测到这些BC，导致回归问题被遗漏。

Method: BreakGuard静态提取所有调用目标库方法（调用点）的客户端方法（焦点方法），然后为每个焦点方法生成测试。测试在预破坏版本上通过、在破坏版本上失败时判定为检测到BC。使用3种LLM（GPT4o、Qwen3-coder-480B、GPT-OSS-120B）和三种上下文级别（最小、方法、类）进行评估。

Result: 在BUMP数据集的89个真实BC上，最佳配置检测出30.3%的BC（27/89），平均每个检测到的BC成本约0.90美元。成功检测了不同库类别的BC（JSON库、日志、解析等），但LLM生成的测试对崩溃型BC的检测比行为型BC更可靠。

Conclusion: BreakGuard验证了LLM驱动测试生成在检测客户端库破坏性变更方面的可行性，但同时也揭示了其局限性——对行为型BC的检测能力有限，未来需要改进以提升对行为型变更的覆盖。

Abstract: Open-source libraries play an important role in software development by providing reusable features that expedite the development process. As libraries evolve, they release new versions that add features, fix bugs, or apply security patches. In this process, they may break the contract established with their clients by introducing breaking changes (BCs) that alter the runtime behavior and break client applications. Client-side test suites often fail to detect these BCs because of limited library coverage that does not exercise all library methods used in the client's codebase.
  We propose BreakGuard, an approach that generates a test suite to detect breaking changes in clients. BreakGuard statically extracts every client method (focal method) that invokes the target library method (call site), then generates tests per focal method. A test detects a BC if it passes on the pre-breaking version and fails on the breaking version. We evaluate our approach on 89 real-world breaking changes from the BUMP dataset, using 3 LLMs (GPT4o, Qwen3-coder-480B, GPT-OSS-120B) and three context levels: minimal, method, and class. Using the best-performing configuration, BreakGuard detects 30.3% of breaking changes (27 of 89) at a mean cost of roughly $0.90 USD per detected breaking change. We successfully detected BCs from different library categories (e.g., JSON libraries, logging, parsing), but we find LLM-generated tests to be more reliable for detecting crash-type breaking changes as opposed to behavioural BCs.

</details>


### [28] [From Agent Behaviour to Agent-Friendly Documentation: An Empirical Study of How Coding Agents Discover, Read, and Write Technical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20195)
*Zhijun Gao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对自主编码代理与软件文档的交互行为进行了实证研究，基于两个公开数据集（557个代理编码会话和33,097个代理pull requests），发现四项挑战当前文档实践的关键发现：代理文档工作以面向代理的工件为主（指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%）；文档查阅与代码编辑之间的关系不明确；缺乏基于文档的验证序列且查阅与即时测试负相关；文档查阅多为自发起而非故障驱动，且文档滞后于代码。研究提出代理-文档交互的"双叶循环"描述模型，并指出"可操作性"和"可验证性"这两个"代理友好"文档的常见假设缺乏一致的行为支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术文档传统上为人开发者编写，但越来越多的软件变更由自主编码代理完成。然而，代理在何时查阅哪些文档、以及查阅后的行为后果完全未知。这一知识空白直接威胁到文档实践的有效性，因此需要通过行为层面的实证研究来理解代理-文档交互模式。

Method: 采用行为扎根（behavior-grounded）研究方法，分析两个公开数据集：SWE-chat（557个代理编码会话，94,813个开发事件，含3,033次文档交互）和AIDev（33,097个代理pull requests，690,260条文件级变更记录）。通过相邻转移概率、lift值以及阶段调整（stage-adjusted）模型（OR值）等统计指标，对文档查阅、代码编辑、测试验证等行为间的关联进行量化分析。

Result: 四项核心发现：(1) 代理的文档工作以面向代理的工件为主，指令文件和工作笔记占60.5%，而经典技术文档仅占10.6%、API参考仅占1.3%；(2) 文档查阅与代码编辑的关联不明确，未调整的相邻转移概率为0.002、三事件lift为1.05，但阶段调整模型显示OR=1.33（95% CI 1.09-1.62）；文档创建在未调整时lift=1.67但调整后区间包含1；(3) 未观察到明确的基于文档的验证序列，查阅与即时测试负相关（lift 0.23，调整后OR=0.39）；(4) 文档查阅70.2%为自发起而非故障驱动（7.5%），且在多提交pull requests中代码先于文档被修改的频率是文档先行的4.7倍。

Conclusion: 代理-文档交互应被理解为"双叶循环"（two-lobed cycle）而非线性旅程。当前广泛假设的"代理友好"文档的两个属性——可操作性（actionability）和可验证性（verifiability）——缺乏一致的行为证据支持。研究提供了公开的流水线、编码方案和事件级数据，为后续研究者建立了可复用的分析基础设施。

Abstract: Technical documentation is written for human developers, but an increasing share of software changes is now authored by autonomous coding agents. Which documents they consult, when, and what follows remain unknown. We conduct a behaviour-grounded study of agent-documentation interaction across two public datasets: 557 agentic coding sessions from SWE-chat, yielding 94,813 development events including 3,033 documentation interactions; and 33,097 agentic pull requests from AIDev, with 690,260 classified file-level change records. Four findings challenge current documentation practice. First, agents' documentation work is dominated by agent-facing artefacts: instruction files and working notes account for 60.5% of all documentation interactions, versus 10.6% for classical technical documentation and 1.3% for API references. Second, the link between consultation and code editing is unresolved: the adjacent transition probability is 0.002 and the unadjusted three-event lift 1.05, whereas a stage-adjusted model places it above unity (OR 1.33 [1.09, 1.62]); documentation creation is elevated unadjusted (lift 1.67) but its adjusted interval includes unity. Third, no explicit documentation-based validation sequence was observed, and consultation is associated with less immediate testing (lift 0.23, cluster CI 0.08-0.45; adjusted OR 0.39 [0.25, 0.60]). Fourth, consultation is self-initiated (70.2%) far more often than failure-driven (7.5%), and documentation trails code: among multi-commit pull requests changing both, code is touched first 4.7x more often. From these traces we derive a descriptive model of agent-documentation interaction as a two-lobed cycle rather than a linear journey, and show that two widely assumed properties of "agent-friendly" documentation - actionability and verifiability - lack consistent behavioural support. We release our pipeline, coding scheme, and event-level data.

</details>

### [29] [Rationally Enriched Chebyshev Trunk Bases for DeepONet Surrogates of High Péclet Entrance Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19658)
*Mingeun Choi,Satish Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有理增强切比雪夫（REC）trunk用于深度算子网络（DeepONet），以改进对具有薄边界/壁层的奇异摄动和高Péclet输运问题的代理建模。REC trunk结合切比雪夫多项式基与AAA算法构造的有理基元素，在三个测试问题中相比vanilla DeepONet和纯Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet，在预测标量轮廓、壁法向温度和浓度轮廓方面均实现误差降低，最高相对改善分别达19.5%、60.2%和32.2%，并有效抑制了近壁振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异摄动和高Péclet输运问题的解具有极薄的局部边界层或壁层，传统DeepONet（如vanilla或纯Chebyshev trunk）难以准确捕捉这类陡峭梯度特征，导致预测误差大且出现人工近壁振荡。因此需要设计更有效的trunk字典来提升DeepONet对这类问题的逼近能力。

Method: 提出REC trunk，将Chebyshev多项式字典元素与基于自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法构造的有理字典元素相结合，用于DeepONet的trunk网络。在三个问题（奇异摄动标量BVP、壁面温度热入口问题、吸收壁浓度入口问题）上，与vanilla DeepONet和仅含Chebyshev多项式的Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet进行对比评估，每个设置执行5次独立训练。

Result: 在保留测试轮廓上：REC-trunk DeepONet相比vanilla DeepONet有显著改进，与Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet在标量轮廓预测上相当；当摄动参数在1.00×10⁻⁴至1.78×10⁻⁴区间内时，REC-trunk相对Chebyshev-trunk最多降低19.5%的轮廓误差指标。在壁法向温度和浓度轮廓预测上，分别相对vanilla和Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet降低最多60.2%和32.2%的误差，并在Péclet或传质Péclet数范围为10²至10⁴时抑制了人工近壁振荡。

Conclusion: REC-trunk DeepONet通过引入AAA算法构造的有理字典元素，有效提升了DeepONet对具有薄边界/壁层结构的奇异摄动和高Péclet输运问题的代理建模精度，尤其在中等摄动参数区间和壁法向轮廓预测上优势明显，同时避免了近壁伪振荡，是一种具有实用价值的改进方案。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a rationally enriched Chebyshev (REC) trunk for deep operator network (DeepONet) surrogate models of singularly perturbed and high-Péclet transport problems whose solution profiles are characterized by thin localized boundary or wall layers. The REC trunk combines Chebyshev polynomial dictionary elements with rational dictionary elements constructed using the adaptive Antoulas-Anderson (AAA) algorithm. Over five independent training runs, the resulting REC-trunk DeepONet is evaluated against a vanilla DeepONet and a Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet whose prescribed dictionary consists only of Chebyshev polynomials across three problems whose singular perturbation parameters are diffusion-to-advection ratios: a singularly perturbed scalar boundary-value problem (BVP), the thermal entrance problem with a prescribed wall temperature, and the concentration entrance problem with an absorbing wall. Across the held-out test profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet improves over the vanilla DeepONet and remains comparable to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet in predicting the scalar profile, with its clearest advantage over the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet appearing when the perturbation parameter lies between $1.00\times10^{-4}$ and $1.78\times10^{-4}$, where it reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $19.5\,\%$ relative to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet. In predicting the wall-normal temperature and concentration profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $60.2\,\%$ and $32.2\,\%$ relative to the vanilla and Chebyshev-trunk DeepONets, respectively, while suppressing artificial near-wall oscillations as the Péclet or mass-transfer Péclet number ranges from $10^{2}$ to $10^{4}$.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究展示了在满足航空领域学习保证标准的前提下，LSTM递归神经网络能够有效部署于传统航电硬件上，为机载关键功能提供可靠的重量估计能力，为机器学习在航空安全关键系统中的应用提供了实践范例。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [31] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三角模糊数距离度量d_{TR}，该度量将线性重缩放（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，从而在比较模糊数时自动完成归一化，无需预处理阶段。作者证明了该度量满足度量的所有性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于异构模糊数据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现有的模糊距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时，需要预先进行归一化处理，这一额外步骤既繁琐又可能引入误差。本文旨在设计一种无需预归一化即可直接处理异构模糊数据的距离度量。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离（d_{TR}），其核心创新在于将线性重缩放（LRE）嵌入距离计算公式内部，使归一化在距离计算过程中自动完成。该方法还结合了权重向量以优先处理不同维度，并给出了完整的数学证明以验证其度量性质。

Result: 严格证明了d_{TR}满足度量的全部四个性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并进一步证明了它具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。这些性质使得d_{TR}在异构模糊数据应用中具有理论保障。

Conclusion: d_{TR}为异构模糊数据下的距离度量提供了一种无需预归一化的解决方案，可广泛应用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等领域，填补了现有方法在处理不同尺度模糊数据时的空白。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>
